; 独占个人定时器98；每秒维护无攻击倒计时、四点轮换及切图后挂机恢复。
[@OnTimer98]
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_PATROL_ACTIVE 0
#ACT
SetOffTimer 98
BREAK

#IF
EQUAL N$XY_PATROL_RESTART 1
#ACT
MOV N$XY_PATROL_RESTART 0
StartAutoPlayGame
SENDMSG 6 [玄渊巡航] 已到达巡航点，自动挂机继续运行。
BREAK

#IF
LARGE N$XY_PATROL_CD 0
#ACT
DEC N$XY_PATROL_CD 1

#IF
EQUAL N$XY_PATROL_CD 0
EQUAL N$XY_PATROL_INDEX 1
#ACT
MOV N$XY_PATROL_INDEX 2
MOV N$XY_PATROL_CD 60
MOV N$XY_PATROL_RESTART 1
SENDMSG 6 [玄渊巡航] 60秒未攻击，前往一号巡航点。

BREAK
